## change: request -> decision -> work order -> candidate -> accepted

### POST /v1/projects/order-operations-portal/changes
request_body:
{
  "actor": "founder-001",
  "reason": "EB-017: the healthz identity change that this promotion ships",
  "request": {
    "title": "Report revision and build digest from /healthz",
    "objective": "Let a local instance answer which build runs and which start of it answers, so a promotion gate can read it back without a container registry.",
    "owner_summary": "Each local instance reports its environment label, a per-start revision and the sha256 of its own executable on /healthz.",
    "scope_included": [
      "healthz identity fields",
      "unit tests"
    ],
    "scope_excluded": [
      "project.yaml",
      "authentication",
      "order review behaviour"
    ],
    "acceptance_criteria": [
      {
        "text": "healthz reports revision, build_digest and started_at"
      },
      {
        "text": "build_digest equals the sha256 of the running executable"
      }
    ],
    "allowed_paths": [
      "main.go",
      "main_test.go"
    ],
    "forbidden_actions": [
      "project.yaml edits",
      "authentication"
    ],
    "target_environment_id": "staging",
    "business_constraints": {
      "data_classification": "internal",
      "expected_monthly_volume": "500 reviews"
    },
    "requested_by": "founder-001"
  }
}
